Montella describes ‘dream’ Milan opportunity, Berlusconi and the Supercoppa: “An incredible feeling”

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Vincenzo Montella’s time with AC Milan was ‘a dream’ for him, but he failed to achieve much during his time at the helm.

Looking back on Montella’s time leading Milan is tricky. On paper, the Italian had a strong tenure at the club, achieving a 50% win rate and only losing 18 of his 64 games in charge at San Siro, not a terrible record by any means.

Read SempreMilan ad-free and get access to exclusive news. Click here for a free trial!

He even ended a five-year trophy drought with a win against Juventus in the Supercoppa Italiana and returned the Rossoneri to Europe, with a sixth-place finish in the 2016/17 season. In itself, that was a first since 2014.

This is where context matters, though. The start of the 2017/18 season was tough, as the Diavolo won six of their first 14 games and he was sacked before December. Despite that, he still views the club in an excellent light.

Speaking to La Gazzetta dello Sport’s ‘La Tripletta’ podcast, Montella went over his time with the club and how he fulfilled a dream by taking the job, as MilanPress relays.

“It was a fantastic experience there [at Milan] too, it was my dream. Then, as a professional, it’s a little different, but when I was thirteen I was a fan of that AC Milan team. My older brother took me to see Maradona’s Napoli team twice to make me a Napoli fan, and Maradona scored three goals.

“Despite this, my idol was Van Basten because I was a striker: I studied him and dreamed of becoming like him, and so I was a Milan fan. I knew everything about Milan, so for me it was an incredible feeling to join Milan.”

“He liked to talk about football; he liked to give advice even when it was a bit out of context at the time, because he always had the mentality of having Van Basten as a centre forward with Papin, a two-time Ballon d’Or winner, on the bench. He wanted me to play with two strikers, and I tried to plug the gap for months.

“I told him it was difficult; I knew all the lineups of those years. I was a Milan fan: the starter was Van Basten and the reserve was Papin, both Ballon d’Or winners. So, ‘If you buy me some players like that, rest assured that I’ll play them both.'”

“It was a year when we returned to Europe. I think it had been two or three years since Milan had been there, and it was something new at the time. We won a trophy, a small one. Just the other night I saw Galliani at a dinner; he reminded me of it and remembers it perfectly.

“I have to say that I found him to be the perfect manager at the time, because he was always on my side when it came to pressure; he gave me complete autonomy in my decisions and he helped me with everything, from the number of collaborators to new things; whatever I asked for, he was always on the coach’s side.

“And so for me he was truly an incredible manager, who in that phase, in that change, was fundamental even in achieving these small results, especially after Berlusconi’s great AC Milan.

“We deserved to win even in regulation time: we had some easy chances to win it, then it came to penalties. Gigio saved the penalty from Dybala, who kicked it really hard, but he filled the net with it.”

“I was a little worried from the beginning because there was this obsession with publicising everything, almost like a publicity stunt, so I was a little worried. In theory, there was sharing, at least that’s what it looked like, but I couldn’t back out when in reality that wasn’t always the case.

“And so sometimes there were players I hadn’t even consulted about, who would arrive and then say, ‘It was shared,’ and when you’re there, you can’t say otherwise. Certainly, when you build a team and have the budget, the first thing you do is: we had a goalkeeper, I’ll get a centre forward, we were missing a centre forward.

“In the final selection, the centre forward was the only one left, with a budget of 15-20 million. And then Kalinic… yes, I chose him because that was the final budget. So our, let’s say, second-choice striker was paid thirty-eight million, if I remember correctly, who was André Silva, and the first striker was paid half that.

“And so, in my opinion, a misunderstanding of this kind was created, with so many changes, even though at that stage we weren’t even playing badly for having fifteen new players.

“We still had Calhanoglu, who wasn’t feeling well, coming off months of suspension. The team was still being built: we weren’t in line with the Scudetto objectives that had been announced, but certainly, given our development, we were absolutely on target.

“Who would I have signed up front? At the time, we were probably negotiating for Aubameyang, if I remember correctly. Ronaldo? No, I didn’t believe it, not at all.”

CAN 2025: Senegal-Morocco, huge drama after the CAS verdict!

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

The series of the CAN 2025 final is not yet over. A few hours after the decision of the Court of Arbitration for Sport (CAS), which confirmed Senegal’s victory against Morocco, the Royal Moroccan Football Federation (FRMF) officially reacted. In a particularly firm statement, the Moroccan body expressed its disagreement with this decision and announced its intention to initiate new legal procedures.

“The Royal Moroccan Football Federation expresses its protest against the fact that its request was not taken into consideration”indicates in particular the press release published this Tuesday. The FRMF considers that its arguments concerning the alleged irregularities of the procedure have not been sufficiently examined by the sports court. It considers in particular that certain elements call into question “the integrity and credibility of the decision. »

To support its protest, the FRMF highlights a specific element: “It was confirmed that contact had taken place between the German referee, Ulrich Haas, and the president of the Senegalese Football Federation, 48 hours before the decision was given. » The Moroccan Federation believes that this situation should have been taken into consideration when examining the file.

The Moroccan body also contests the interpretation adopted by the CAS concerning the temporary departure of the Senegalese players from the field during the final. She believes that the decision “still does not take into account the need to comply with the direction enshrined in recent decisions of the International Football Association Board (IFAB) and the Fédération Internationale de Football Association (FIFA). »

The FRMF particularly emphasizes the regulatory consequences of the behavior of players who leave the field without the authorization of the referee. She thus evokes “the resulting responsibilities and disciplinary sanctions”considering that this aspect was not correctly taken into account in the decision rendered in Lausanne.

Despite the CAS verdict, the Moroccan Federation does not intend to turn the page. She clearly announced her desire to continue the process: “As a result, the Royal Moroccan Football Federation has decided to initiate all necessary legal procedures to challenge the CAS decision. »

As a reminder, the Court of Arbitration for Sport annulled the decision of the CAF Appeal Jury, which had awarded a victory on the green carpet to Morocco (3-0). He reinstated the decision of the Disciplinary Jury and confirmed the result obtained on the field, a victory for Senegal (1-0). The reaction of the FRMF now opens a new sequence in this particularly tense issue between the two federations.

Ex-refs chief hits out at Dermot Gallagher's 'incorrect' Sky TV verdict as Man United decision overturned

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

The FA have rightly overturned Leny Yoro's red card after he was given his marching orders in Man United's defeat at Liverpool.

That is according to former PGMOL chief and FIFA referee, Keith Hackett, who believes that the incident should force Howard Webb to advise the officials involved about DOGSO rules.

Yoro saw red inside the first eight minutes as Mauricio Pochettino's first game in charge of the Red Devils ended in a 4-0 loss at Anfield on Saturday.

The French centre-back pulled Alexander Isak down just outside of the penalty area and was adjudged to have denied the Reds striker a goalscoring opportunity.

Referee Sam Barrott produced the red card without hesitation, while VAR officials John Brooks and Paul Howard were content to see the decision stand.

The FA have now gone against their verdict and overturned the call, with Yoro now clear to face Brentford in Premier League action next weekend.

There was a clear element of debate around Yoro's red card, given that Isak did not look to be in control of the ball when he was fouled following a heavy touch.

Pro Ref chief Webb backed his officials by insisting that it was the "correct" decision to send the United defender off on Sky Sports' Mic'd Up show.

The FA have taken a different view and removed the sanction from his record, which Hackett believes is the right outcome.

Speaking exclusively to Football Insider, he said: "The [DOGSO] criteria was not fulfilled, i.e. likelihood of keeping or gaining control of the ball. Having watched Dermot Gallagher on Ref Watch stating that the decision by the referee was correct, it was evident that he had failed to refer to law.

"I advised that Manchester Utd should appeal. The appeals panel have come to the correct decision by overturning the red card. I suggest that Howard Webb and his group of match officials need to deliver some operational advice so that, in future, the law is applied correctly."

Yoro made a mistake against Liverpool that ultimately contributed to United's heavy loss, but Pochettino must keep him in the side for the time being.

He has been in and out of the team so far this term, but ranks highly among several key metrics among top-flight centre-backs up to now.

Yoro has a 90 per cent successful pass rate (93rd percentile) from 41.7 successful passes (77th percentile) per 90 minutes, as well as 15 line-breaking passes (90th percentile) per 90.

He also has 10.6 defensive contributions (96th percentile) per 90, with 3.01 tackles (88th percentile), 3.21 interceptions (97th percentile) and 4.21 clearances (91st percentile) per 90.

Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund LIVE: Champions League match stream, latest team news, lineups, TV, prediction

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Arsenal take on Borussia Dortmund this evening as they return to action in the Champions League. It’s the Premier League champions against the Bundesliga leaders tonight, and Mikel Arteta’s side will be hoping to produce a statement performance to continue their unbeaten run on the continent.

The Gunners have played four and won three of their games so far in Europe, drawing the other. Last time out, they eked past Slavia Prague 2-1 thanks to goals from Noni Madueke and Viktor Gyokeres, while in the league, they stuttered to a late 2-2 draw with Newcastle United as Madueke struck an equaliser in the dying embers to snatch a point.

Niko Kovac’s visitors, meanwhile sit atop the German top flight, two points clear of ‘Klassiker’ rivals Bayern Munich after 10 matches. They are fresh off a narrow 1-0 win at Borussia Monchengladbach over the weekend, as Felix Nmecha notched the winner in the 80th minute at Borussia-Park. Keep up to date with all the latest from the Emirates Stadium with our live blog below.

Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund latest updates

In the FA Cup, loan players are prohibited from playing against their parent clubs, whilst in the Premier League, the rules state a “player shall not play against the transferor [parent] club unless the transferor club and the transferee [loan] club agree in writing that the player can play against the transferor club.”

UEFA rules state that clubs cannot exert "any influence whatsoever over the players that another club may field in a match". This means that Arsenal could not add a clause to Nwaneri’s loan deal preventing the teenager from facing his parent club.

Ultimately it may be a moot point regarding Nwaneri’s eligibility to face his permanent employers. The 19-year-old has struggled for game time since joining Dortmund on a temporary basis, with Nwaneri last starting for Dortmund in mid-October.

Mikel Arteta has warned Ethan Nwaneri that "moments are not enough" if he is to turn his Borussia Dortmund loan around.

The young forward joined the German club in the summer on loan from Arsenal and he was initially handed opportunities in the starting lineup by Niko Kovac.

However, those have been few and far between in recent weeks. Nwaneri has not started a match in over a month and has played just five minutes of football across Dortmund's last three league matches.

Any and all updates on David Raya were welcome if you were an Arsenal fan, Matt Verri writes.

There were the attempts to lip read what head physio Simon Murphy said as he walked off the St James’ Park pitch with Raya.

Then the sighting of Raya the following day in Oxford Circus with his arm in a sling. And the report that he had been able to drive himself home after the club returned to London from Newcastle.

Mikel Arteta is facing a tricky decision between the sticks as Arsenal face Borussia Dortmund in the Champions League tonight, with usual No1 David Raya ruled out.

Raya was forced off with a shoulder injury during Arsenal’s 2-2 draw with Newcastle United in the Premier League. Ruled out of tonight’s game at the Emirates, Arsenal fans are now wondering who will start in Raya’s stead.

Arsenal’s goalkeeping pecking order has been established by Mikel Arteta since the start of the campaign. When available, Kepa Arrizabalaga has been named on the bench - only missing the game with Nottingham Forest due to a knock.

Kepa’s introduction as Raya’s replacement on Saturday further cements his position as first-choice backup, despite having played the same amount of games as Meslier prior to the weekend.

Bruno Guimaraes has revealed the one piece of advice Declan Rice gave him that helped him settle after joining Arsenal.

The Brazilian midfielder sealed an acrimonious move from Newcastle to north London in August after an extended transfer saga, and had fresh silverware to his name almost immediately after beating Manchester City in the Community Shield.

A tricky one considering Dortmund’s form, and Arsenal’s shakiness against Newcastle, but the Gunners should win this one with the quality at the their disposal.

For the visitors, they are missing Emre Can, who is out with an ACL tear, and the same can be said for highly-rated summer signing Giannis Konstantelias.

Samuele Inacio, Justin Lerma and Mussa Kaba are all youngsters on the fringes of the first team who are also out injured.

Arsenal have a huge Premier League match at home to Manchester City to come on Sunday but Bundesliga leaders Dortmund represent tough opposition and manager Mikel Arteta is likely to go strong with his team selection.

That would mean more minutes in midfield for Declan Rice and captain Martin Odegaard, with Martin Zubimendi still sidelined, while Noni Madueke has become an essential part of the attack on the right wing in the absence of Bukayo Saka.

Up front, Viktor Gyokeres' chances to start have largely come in the Champions League this season and it would be no surprise if he was chosen to lead the line against Dortmund, ahead of Kai Havertz.

Goalkeeper David Raya will miss the match with a shoulder injury suffered late on during Saturday’s 2-2 draw with Newcastle in the Premier League and deputy Kepa Arrizabalaga is expected to start in his place, over Illan Meslier.

Ben White could get the nod at right-back, as he continues to share minutes with Jurrien Timber, while Myles Lewis-Skelly will hope to get his opportunity in midfield.

William Saliba is in contention to make his first Arsenal start of the season against Borussia Dortmund, as Mikel Arteta also faces a tricky decision over who starts in goal.

The French defender had a long spell on the sidelines after aggravating a back injury at the World Cup over the summer.

Saliba did not require surgery but a significant period of rest was needed and he is yet to play a single first-team minute so far this season.

He played the first half for Arsenal’s Under-21s in their PL2 win over Aston Villa before the international break and then featured in a behind-closed-doors friendly against Watford.

Having come through those games without any issues, Saliba is in contention for a first-team return and he could be brought in against Dortmund on Tuesday night.

Rob Gronkowski, Adrian Peterson, Ben Roethlisberger among players advancing in Hall of Fame voting process

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

The Pro Football Hall of Fame announced the names of 52 players who have advanced to the next round of voting for possible enshrinement as part of the Class of 2027.

The group includes five players who are in their first year of eligibility. They are tight end Rob Gronkowski, running back Adrian Peterson, quarterback Ben Roethlisberger, wide receiver Antonio Brown, and cornerback Richard Sherman.

After changes to the selection process this year, there are no longer Modern and Senior candidates in the player pool. The group of 52 players will be cut to 25 in the next round of voting and then 13 finalists will be selected from that group. They will join Bill Belichick and Robert Kraft in the final pool of eligible candidates with the selection committee selecting a group of between three and seven members.

The full list of players who have advanced to the next round of voting appearsa below.

OFFENSIVE LINEMEN: Willie Anderson, Lomas Brown, Jahri Evans, Joe Jacoby, Nick Mangold, Logan Mankins, Maurkice Pouncey, Richmond Webb, Steve Wisniewski, Marshal Yanda.

QUARTERBACKS: Ken Anderson, Eli Manning, Ben Roethlisberger.

RUNNING BACKS: Shaun Alexander, Frank Gore, Marshawn Lynch, LeSean McCoy, Adrian Peterson, Fred Taylor, Ricky Watters.

WIDE RECEIVERS/TIGHT ENDS: Anquan Boldin, Antonio Brown, Rob Gronkowski, Torry Holt, Billy “White Shoes” Johnson, Steve Smith Sr., Otis Taylor, Hines Ward, Reggie Wayne, Jason Witten.

DEFENSIVE BACKS: Rodney Harrison, Lester Hayes, Albert Lewis, Eddie Meador, Richard Sherman, Earl Thomas III, Everson Walls, Darren Woodson.

DEFENSIVE LINEMEN: John Abraham, Geno Atkins, L.C. Greenwood, Jim Marshall, Robert Mathis, Haloti Ngata, Vince Wilfork, Kevin Williams.

LINEBACKERS: Maxie Baughan, London Fletcher, James Harrison, Karl Mecklenburg, Tommy Nobis, Terrell Suggs.

Dybala ‘proud’ of Roma’s Champions League return and claims: ‘I want to end my career in Italy’

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Paulo Dybala says he is proud to have helped bring Roma back to the Champions League, and hopes to finish his career in Italy.

Speaking to UEFA’s official channels, with quotes via CalcioMercato, the Argentine was speaking ahead of Roma’s league-phase trip to Paris Saint-Germain on Wednesday. Kick-off at the Parc des Princes is 21:00 CET [20:00 GMT].

The Giallorossi go into the game with four points after their defeat to Manchester United, while the holders have won all four of their matches so far.

“In my first years here, it was obviously a disappointment not to be able to bring Roma back to the Champions League. I also felt responsible,” Dybala said.

“In my first year, we came close by reaching the Europa League final, but in the years that followed we didn’t do enough to deserve it. Last year, though, I saw a truly determined team. We showed that by keeping clean sheets in the last few games, because we knew qualification didn’t depend only on us until the final matchday.

“It was a moment of great pride for me, because I could see how happy the fans were to be back in the Champions League, just like we were. We want to test ourselves against the best teams in Europe, and now we can.”

Dybala has stayed fit far more often under Gian Piero Gasperini, and he credited the coach’s approach.

“Physically, knee operations aside, I’ve felt very good over the last year, especially muscularly,” he said.

“The coach and his staff, including the fitness coaches who arrived this year, gave me a limited but appropriate workload in pre-season so I could always be available. So far this season, I’ve felt in good shape and I’m very happy.”

Dybala signed a one-year contract until 30 June 2027 in July. Now in his 15th year in Italy, after spells at Palermo and Juventus before moving to Roma in 2022, he made clear he has no intention of leaving.

“I never imagined I’d have a career like this, let alone spend 15 years in Italy. I’ve lived in the south, the north, and now the centre of the country. I’ve been here for almost half my life and I’m very happy,” he said.

“Italy welcomed me with open arms. My daughter was born here, so she’s Italian. Yes, I hope to stay in Italy for the rest of my career. It would be very special for me to dedicate an entire career to Serie A, as well as the year I spent in Serie B, which was very important for me.”

Kristin Juszczyk on the Science and Style Behind Venus Williams’ F1 Look

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

The designer’s custom trench and corset called attention to how little sports science research focuses on women.

Women's Health may earn commission from the links on this page, but we only feature products we believe in. Why Trust Us?

The Formula 1 Las Vegas Grand Prix is one of the most glamorous weekends in sports. With drivers’ global followings and a long list of A-listers in attendance, the event can feel as much like a fashion show as a race.

But while many guests were focused on the designer labels, Kristin Juszczyk had another message in mind: the need for more research on women in sports.

Juszczyk is a self-taught designer whose work began as a hobby and has since made her a force in the industry. The wife of San Francisco 49ers player Kyle Juszczyk, her custom game-day outfits gained traction on social media, and she has gone on to dress high-profile names including Simone Biles, Taylor Swift, and Caitlin Clark. This year, she was also named to TIME’s 100 Most Influential People in Sports.

For the Las Vegas GP, Juszczyk partnered with Gatorade’s Body of Science, an initiative dedicated to advancing the research on women’s sports. One stat drives their research: 6 percent of sports science research focuses on women.

Only 6 percent of published research in sports and exercise journals has focused exclusively on women, per a study published in the Women in Sport and Physical Activity Journal—a staggering gap that has implications for everything from training to recovery, as previously reported by Women’s Health.

Tennis legend Venus Williams, a Gatorade athlete, wore a custom look Juszczyk designed for the paddock red carpet inspired by this staggering statistic.

“Venus is one of the most iconic athletes of our generation, and to have her being in one of my designs, is surreal,” Juszczyk exclusively tells Women’s Health.

When it came to building the design, Juszczyk says she first took inspiration from motorsport fashion while also channeling the ambience a city like Las Vegas brings to the race. She opted to use leather as the base material, feeling like it channeled the energy of the sport. From there, she designed a trench coat with a layering corset piece over it, an homage to the city’s glitz and glamour.

Designing a trench coat was a first for Juszczyk, but the corset design is what she describes as her personal “bread and butter.” True to the Gatorade brand, the trench featured piping in its iconic orange, offset with black.

That 6 percent statistic is also front and center, ensuring the message of the design is clear: There needs to be more research dedicated toward women’s sports.

Juszczyk wanted to stay true to Venus and her personal style, and did a lot of research on what she wears on the regular. She says she approaches all of her designs with intentionality, ensuring the client is wearing something they feel good in.

“I want it to feel like my design, but I still want it to be something they feel beautiful and confident in,” she says.

The look was on full display when Venus presented the winner of the F1 Academy race, Alisha Palmowski, with her trophy. The moment was symbolic: a titan of women’s sports celebrating the new generation, while calling attention to a research gap that directly affects them.

“If anyone can take anything away from it, it’s that women need as much research in sports as men do,” she says. “The world is doing so much in pushing women athletes forward, and science should back it as well.”

Alyssa Cruz is the social media editor at Women’s Health, where she drives social strategy and helps shape the brand’s voice across platforms. Prior to WH, she worked at CBS News and palabra. Originally from Toledo, she is a proud alumna of Ohio University, where she received two bachelor’s degrees in journalism and Spanish. When she is not creating social content, you can find her trying different restaurants in New York City or planning her next trip.

Manchester United win appeal over Yoro’s red card despite Webb backing decision

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

  • Player now eligible to face Brentford on Saturday

  • Referees’ chief Webb said it was ‘certainly not clearly wrong’

Manchester United have successfully appealed against Leny Yoro’s red card in their 4-0 defeat to Liverpool on the day referees’ chief Howard Webb gave his backing to the original decision. The defender was dismissed for denying an obvious goalscoring opportunity (Dogso) after eight minutes, but this was found to be incorrect.

The Frenchman will no longer be required to serve a one-match suspension after a ruling by an independent panel. He fouled Alexander Isak when the Liverpool forward was through on goal in the early stages at Anfield but Mauricio Pochettino, United’s new head coach, insisted the striker would not have reached the ball, and therefore was not a sending-off offence. The referee Sam Barrott made a different assessment, for which the video assistant referee did not seek a review, a judgment supported by Pro Ref’s chief refereeing officer, Webb.

How To Get The Benefactor Schafter S Legendary Car In GTA 6

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Here is how to get the Guardia Brothers FRA legendary assault rifle and Benefactor car in GTA 6.

There are a lot of secrets and collectibles hidden throughout Grand Theft Auto 6. These can range from simple easter eggs to comically elaborate puzzles you'll need to solve. However, some just require a bit of criminal activity to earn some great rewards.

Two of these are the Guardia Brothers FRA legendary assault rifle and Benefactor sports car, both of which are obtainable at the same location! If you're looking to snag a few grand, a new car, and a weapon, here is where to find them and how to get these legendary items.

If you want to get this legendary assault rifle and sports car, you will need to first head to Vice City and then go to the Little Cuba district. In the northeast corner of Little Cuba there will be a gated house that a black van will pull into if you get close. This doesn't always happen, and your ability to rob this area isn't determined by the van showing up, so don't fret.

While you can try to just bust through the front gate, you can actually sneak in over the wall. Head to the right of the gate along the spiked wall (shown above) until you reach a section near the back that can be climbed over. I strongly recommend doing this, as you can basically eliminate every gang member without raising an alarm so long as you have a silenced weapon.

Once you reach the backyard, ignore the legendary Benefactor car for now and head up the scaffolding along the side of the building to the roof. Climb through the open hatch and equip your silenced weapon. Below you will be four gang members sitting around a table on the ground floor. Additionally, there is one other criminal rummaging through a safe on the second floor.

Take out your silenced weapon, activate Focus mode, and eliminate all four criminals on the first floor before they can react. If you're fast enough, all of them can be killed without alerting anyone outside or even the guy looking in the safe! With the four gang members dead, use your Focus and look at the floor by the ladder.

This will reveal a secret stash area that has some cash you can loot. There is also a high-end watch on the table to your left along with some Sticky Bombs, which you will need to escape. When you're ready, head down to the second floor and knock out the guy putting money into the safe. Take all the cash and go to the bottom floor.

There you will find a mix of money, marked bills, and jewelry. Stuff your bag full of valuables, loot the bodies, and look for a small armory to the right of the front entrance. In a weapon case, you will find the Guardian Brothers FMA assault rifle. Once you've thoroughly ransacked the place, take everything out the back door to where the Benefactor is.

Store your items in the truck of the car, take out your silenced weapon, and kill the guard who is standing along the driveway near the gate. With him dead, approach the gate at the end of the driveway, attach a sticky bomb to the side when prompted, and shoot the two guards on the other side of the gate. Blow open the gate, then run up to the switch to open the main gate so you can drive out (circled above).

This should allow you to escape without triggering a single gunfight or having the police called on you. Now you can take the Benefactor and get it registered at someone like Moses for a little over $3,800. This ensures you can call the car anytime you need it, making it yours for the rest of the game.

Leeds United Can Convince Celtic to Sell 'Best Striker' Durán with Right Offer

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Leeds United may be able to tempt a cash-hungry Celtic board into selling a prize asset if they are willing to spend a significant chunk. The Whites are said to be interested in Celtic frontman Camilo Durán, who has dazzled since joining the Scottish club in the summer.

Durán joined the Hoops for a bargain £5.5million deal from Qarabag after netting five times in the Champions League last season, and he has hit the ground running. The forward has scored 14 goals across all competitions, and he has found the net just as regularly in the Europa League as in the Scottish Premiership.

He has even been a consistent scorer when starting on the right wing, where he has often found himself to make room for centre-forward Kasper Hogh, who arrived on an £11million deal but has only scored three times and is now more than three months without a goal.

And don't be fooled by Celtic's competition. The Hoops signed right-winger Haissem Hassan for £6million - which could raise to £9million - during the summer window, with the Egypt international having impressed during the World Cup, but he is without a goal up to this point, so even other higher quality players are not having the success in front of goal from the same positions Durán is operating in. And that's a big part of the reason clubs like Leeds are said to be scouting the Colombian.

But Celtic's reliance on Durán means it's going to be hard to lure the Colombian away from Parkhead, especially during the season. A January deal is going to be particularly difficult to strike, although there may be a slither of hope if Celtic are eliminated from the Europa League before then. The Bhoys currently have three points after four games and will need six or seven points from their last four to extend their European campaign.

That could be a key factor, but in any case, a summer deal remains realistic if Leeds are willing to pay north of £15million, which would give Celtic the chance of tripling their £5.5million investment in the space of just one year.

"I believe he is the future of Colombian football and he should be first pick in our team. For me, he is the best striker we have." - Arnulfo Valentierra (Durán's former Independiente Medellín manager) speaking this season.

This Celtic board has previously turned down big money in the January window, blocking the £25million exit of Arne Engels last January, but they sold him for £3million less in the summer when West Ham came calling. They never replaced the midfielder, either, leaving fans furious amid an ongoing civil war between them and the board over a perceived lack of ambition.

“We are building a team here and guys like Camilo are part of the future. He has a lot of talent.” - Nestor Lorenzo (Colombia head coach)

This Celtic board likes to turn a profit, and it is willing to do it at the cost of the team to a certain degree. Another sale could complicate things, and Benjamin Nygren is the leading candidate to leave next summer as things stand, but if Leeds can get in early and make a sizable offer for Durán, the bargain deal he arrived at Celtic on will give them a chance of sealing a deal, as long as they are willing to stump up a sizable amount of cash compared to that initial £5.5million fee.

Camilo Duran has been in electric form for Celtic and Leeds United have been scouting the 14-goal man this season

Presenting: Ben Shelton and Trinity Rodman’s Relationship Timeline Amid Reports They’ve Possibly Split

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

For the past two years, one of the sport’s brightest young stars, Ben Shelton, has been in a relationship with Washington Spirit soccer champ Trinity Rodman—and despite their busy schedules, they seemed to be head over heels in love with each other. That is, until November 24, when the Daily Mail broke the news that the power couple reportedly split.

Since you’re probs wondering how this couple came to be (and what led to their rumored breakup), I’ve done the work of rounding up all of their biggest relationship moments in a little timeline for your pleasure. Read it and weep!

Ben hard launches their relationship on Instagram by sneaking a photo of Trinity kissing his cheek into a carousel post. Swipe to the end to see:

Soon after, Trinity clears up some of the discourse around her and Ben’s relationship—namely the story behind how they actually met—and makes a point to note that Frances Tiafoe, a friend of hers and Ben’s, did not introduce them to each other like he previously claimed.

“I can assure you Frances was not the reason we met or got together,” she said in an interview with ESPN. “I don’t know why he’s taking credit for that. Me and Ben can both cut that out. It was a solely me and Ben thing.”

For the record, their story went a little like this: “I was like, ‘Hey, when you gonna come visit me?’ and he was like, ‘I’ll come right now,’” Trinity explained in an Instagram video (below). “So then he flew out and the rest is history.”

By the end of the month, Ben appears on Trinity’s Instagram. In the photo, she leans back into Ben’s shoulder, while his face stays mysteriously hidden beneath a hat (despite her tagging him in the photo). Her caption? “Dibs 🤭.”

Ben also chimes in on clearing up the story on how he and Trinity got together, telling the New York Post, “Tiafoe had nothing to do with it. He’s taking credit, [saying] ‘Oh, yeah, I put them together. I was like Danny Ainge.’ He’s a liar. He’s trying to make it about himself. It’s not about him, but that’s funny.”

Ben gives Trinity prime placement on his feed with a super cute photo:

A few weeks later, Ben wishes Trinity a happy birthday on his Instagram page with a simple but sweet message that reads, “happy birthday beautiful girl, I love you.” Awwwww.

Trinity later gives her followers an even better look into her and Ben’s relationship, sharing a compilation video of them sweetly holding hands during their adventures.

Trinity is spotted in the stands with Ben’s family at Wimbledon.

And she later calls out Wimbledon commentators for mentioning her famous dad, Dennis Rodman, during Ben’s match:

Trinity roots for Ben on day one of the 2025 U.S. Open Tennis Championships, and literally pops out in the cutest ‘fit that consists of Ben’s face plastered all over her custom sweats.

Trinity writes the sweetest tribute message for Ben’s 24th birthday: “To the most beautiful soul I’ve ever met. Happy birthday Benny! You’ve changed me, loving me the way I never knew someone could. Even with your lifestyle you make me a priority every chance you get and I’ll always be grateful for every minute you love me. You inspire me everyday, and you always keep a smile on my face. Life with you is a dream. I love you NL❤️”

And Ben makes an appearance in the comments section, too, writing, “I love you ❤️.”

Trinity’s soccer team, Washington Spirit, is beaten by Gotham FC in a match. Ben consoles her on the side of the soccer pitch like the great boyfriend that he is.

Ben and Trinity’s relationship becomes wedding-guest official! The couple gets all gussied up for a friend’s wedding, and they look so good, it’s literally unfair.

In a lil change of format, Trinity takes her followers along for her swanky date with Ben—and there’s a really intimate moment where Ben pops into frame to kiss her neck (squeal).

Trinity also sits down with Cosmo for an interview and reveals that Ben urged her to temporarily step away from soccer amid her injury.

“When I was going through the injury [this past season]. I was very scared to step away from my team because I was scared they might think, ‘Oh, Trinity just thinks she can do what she wants. She can leave and focus on herself. That's selfish.’ And Ben was like, ‘Your health is everything. You could get hurt and be done playing soccer, then what?’” she recalled. “And so he just reminded me that I need to prioritize myself before it's too late. And he said: ‘You are the driver. If you want to turn left, you're gonna turn left. Do you want to crash or do you want to keep going on the road?’”

She notes that birthdays and large celebrations can be difficult for her and Ben to do on the actual day, likely due to their sporty commitments. She added, “For us, it's so hard to celebrate literally anything. Even for our birthdays, we celebrated them, like the week before and the week after. We celebrate on the phone. Thankfully for preseason, with Spirit, I'll be going to Florida, and he'll be there.”

Ben celebrates Trinity’s birthday with a carousel of images shared to his Instagram page, in true boyfriend style.

Ben plays in—and wins—the ATP 250 tournament. While giving a speech after his impressive title win, Ben publicly thanks Trinity for coming out, and sweetly calls her his good luck charm: “I wanna thank my girlfriend for flying here from Brazil. I won a title at I think every tournament that she's been at this year. You’re definitely the lucky charm.”

That same day, Ben posts a photo roundup to his Instagram, which features Trinity front and center.

Trinity, meanwhile, gives a closer look at her adorable Ben 10 tank top and reminds her followers that she is Ben’s No. 1 fan.

Wimbledon doesn’t go as planned for Ben (he was eliminated in the first round by Otto Virtanen in a shocking upset), but during the U.S. Open, he ends up besting Carlos Alcaraz in a five-set nail-biter.

And ofc, Trinity is there to show him all the love:

The Daily Mail breaks the news that—after almost two years together—Ben and Trinity have reportedly called it quits, noting that “their conflicting work schedules and constant travel made it too challenging for the couple to continue maintaining their relationship.” Luckily, sources add that Ben and Trinity are “believed to remain close.”

Camarda recalls emotion of disallowed UCL goal and Gabbia’s words: “I burst into tears”

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Francesco Camarda has spoken about his Champions League goal that never was today, and how it was an AC Milan dream come true.

Camarda first became known to Milan as a youngster, but he announced himself further when he became Serie A’s youngest-ever appearance maker. He almost then went to a European level, with a goal in the Champions League. However, it wasn’t to be.

Read SempreMilan ad-free and get access to exclusive news. Click here for a free trial!

On his debut in the competition, the starlet bagged a goal which would’ve made him the Rossoneri’s youngest-ever scorer in Europe’s main stage. Yet it was ruled out for offside and a few years on, the now 18-year-old is still waiting for a first competitive goal for the Diavolo.

Speaking to Vice, the striker opened up about his dream of playing in the Champions League at San Siro, his emotions after his goal (and it later being disallowed) and how his teammates rallied around him, as MilanVibes relayed.

“As a child, I used to say to my dad: imagine if one day I could walk into San Siro and hear the Champions League anthem, accompanied by the roar of the entire stadium. When it actually happened, I thought back to that little boy and realised I had realised a dream.

“When I stepped onto the pitch, I had an incredible adrenaline rush. Then the goal came, and I felt the whole stadium explode. I also felt the affection of my teammates, who immediately raised me into the air.

“However, when I looked at the scoreboard and saw that the VAR check was in progress, I was already convinced the goal would be disallowed.

“When I returned home that evening, with my parents and cousin, I burst into tears. I was overjoyed by the goal, but at the same time deeply disappointed by what had happened. It wasn’t easy at first, but I also worked on my mental health and managed to put that moment behind me.

“Today, I’m proud of the journey I’ve taken and of having overcome those difficulties.”

“I remember what Gabbia told me: he was sorry for what happened, but he explained that the experience should give me even more motivation. It should push me to improve, to train harder, and to work so I can relive even more wonderful emotions.”

“In football, there are positive and negative opinions; it has always been this way and will continue to be this way. A player’s true strength lies in not being influenced by what is said, because, regardless of whether you do well or badly, someone will always find a reason to judge you.”

Silver Dawn Emerges from Drydock with New Pool and Dining Concept

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Silversea has unveiled a series of enhancements onboard the Silver Dawn following its recent drydock in Genoa.

They include the addition of Riviera, a new poolside dining, bar and lounge concept first introduced on the Silver Spirit earlier this year, as well as new outdoor experiences.

The company describes Riviera as an open-air concept that is part restaurant, part bar where guests can enjoy drinks, fresh seafood and a mix of daily activities, from morning yoga to evening soirées.

“Silver Dawn’s enhancements reflect our commitment to bringing more options to guests across our fleet,” said Bert Hernandez, president, Silversea.

“With the expansion of Riviera and the addition new outdoor sports, guests have greater variety in how they spend their time onboard. As Silver Dawn sails the Mediterranean and the Caribbean in the coming seasons, guests will find that choice alongside the personalized service that defines Silversea.”

The Riviera concept brings a significant upgrade to the ship’s outdoor guest spaces, bringing new cabanas, loungers, umbrellas, ambient lighting and refreshed furniture layouts.

Guests will also be able to enjoy outdoor sports, with the ship getting three outdoor recreation spaces – a pickleball court, a golf putting area and a golf hitting bay.

The Silver Dawn will welcome its first passengers after drydock on November 25, 2026, a 14-day sailing from Civitavecchia (Rome) to Lisbon through the Mediterranean.

The voyage calls at 10 ports across Italy, Malta, Tunisia, Spain, and Portugal, including Naples, Valletta, Trapani in Sicily, Tunis, Cagliari in Sardinia, Cartagena, Malaga and Cádiz, with overnights in Valletta and Malaga.

Cloaked Named Official Partner of the Dallas Cowboys

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Cloaked to help protect the privacy of the Cowboys Organization through new partnership ahead of Cowboys' Thanksgiving Day game

NEW YORK, Nov. 24, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Cloaked, the consumer-first online privacy company, today announced a partnership with the Dallas Cowboys, naming Cloaked the Official Privacy Partner of the Dallas Cowboys and The Star. By reducing the personal information exposed through data brokers and people-search sites, Cloaked will help protect the Cowboys against social engineering and other targeted attacks.

Fans will see Cloaked throughout AT&T Stadium starting with the Cowboys' Thanksgiving Day home game on Nov. 26. The partnership will also engage the Cowboys' community promoting data privacy, safety, and protection.

"The Dallas Cowboys trusting Cloaked to protect the people behind America's Team means a great deal to us," said Arjun Bhatnagar, co-founder and CEO of Cloaked. "The visibility that makes the Cowboys one of the most recognized organizations in sports also puts the personal information of the people behind the team at risk. But, you don't need to be famous to have your home address, phone number and family details for sale online. If privacy protection matters for America's Team, it matters for everyone. This partnership lets us show millions of Cowboys fans how simple it can be to take back control of their personal information."

Cloaked will also appear across The Star, the Cowboys' headquarters in Frisco, Texas, and on Cowboys preseason television broadcasts and the Dallas Cowboys Radio Network. Fans will also find Cloaked across the Cowboys website, mobile app and social channels, including a recurring game-day series showing how Cloaked helps protect America's Team.

"Visibility comes with being the Dallas Cowboys, and so does the responsibility to protect the people who make this organization what it is," said Matt Messick, Chief Information Officer, Dallas Cowboys. "Personal information available online is exactly what bad actors use to target an organization like ours. Cloaked Enterprise protects people across the Cowboys by removing that information from data brokers and people-search sites, making them harder to find, harder to impersonate and harder to target."

Learn more about Cloaked by visiting www.cloaked.com.

"Cloaked is a consumer-first privacy and security company that helps people take control of their personal information. Cloaked has removed more than 3.5 billion records from data brokers and people-search sites and screened more than 100 million spam and scam calls. Its platform also creates private phone numbers and email addresses to use in place of real ones, and Cloaked Enterprise extends that protection to organizations and their people. Founded by brothers Arjun and Abhijay Bhatnagar, Cloaked has raised more than $400 million from investors including General Catalyst, Liberty City Ventures and Lux Capital. Learn more at www.cloaked.com."

Cavan Sullivan wins MLS Young Player of the Year as league announces award winners and finalists

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

If not listed, please contact your TV provider.

With the MLS Cup playoffs now in full swing, it also means that we are beginning to see who is taking home honors at the end of the season. Nashville SC have already seen Maxwell Woledzi win Defender of the Year and Bran Shwake earn Goalkeeper of the Year honors, and they have potential honorees up and down the awards docket alongside the youngest MVP finalist in league history, Cavan Sullivan, who has taken home young player of the year honors. Antoine Griezmann has also won Newcomer of the Year in only half of a season with Orlando City SC.

Check out the winners and finalists so far:

Leading Nashville SC to a record low of 25 goals conceded and tying Tony Meola's record for the most clean sheets in an MLS season with 16, Brian Schwake has taken home Goalkeeper of the Year honors by a landslide, beating out finalists Matt Turner and Roman Bürki. In only his first season as the full-time starter for Nashville, Schwake took the reins and didn't look back, also debuting for the U.S. Men's National Team due to his success. He also set club records for wins, penalty kick saves (three), save percentage (78.4%), and goals against average (0.76).

It's astounding that Eduard Lowen is playing soccer, considering what the few years have looked like for him. In March of this year, his wife Ilona died from brain cancer after they managed through her diagnosis, with Lowen taking time off from the game to support her and her family. He returned to MLS play in April, scoring in his first start of the season for St. Louis City SC, but then was traded to San Jose after the World Cup break. During that time with San Jose, he became an important part of their playoff run with one goal and three assists.

Becoming the youngest MVP finalist in league history at only 17, it should come as no surprise that Cavan Sullivan has won Young Player of the Year, beating out the Red Bull New York duo of Julian Hall and Adri Mehmeti. During the World Cup break, Sullivan spent time training with the Union's MLS Next Pro side instead of taking the time off for himself, and the results showed in the second half of the season as he helped lead the Union into a playoff spot. With nine goals and 14 assists (including secondary assists), no teenager has ever been involved in more goals in a season than Sullivan, and it has also led to his first cap with the United States men's national team, where he was able to grab his first goal against Mexico. If this is the tip of the iceberg, we're in for something special, considering that this has been a banner year for young players in MLS, with more minutes being given to teenagers than ever before. The future is in good hands as the kids are leveling up each day.

With Guilherme receiving MVP votes to become a finalist for the award, it was going to take a special performance for someone else to win Newcomer of the Year, and joining Orlando City SC, Antoine Griezmann did just that. Prior to his arrival, this was a team floundering below the playoff line after moving on from manager Oscar Pareja. In only a half-season, he came in and scored 14 goals and assisted eight more as the first player ever to produce 20 goal contributions in only half a season, while turning this team into contenders. It's going to be tough in the playoffs for Griezmann to overcome the defense, but considering what he has already done with the squad, there's no reason why Orlando should be counted out with the Frenchman next to Justin Ellis.

En route to setting a points record with 77 points to win the Supporters' Shield, Nashville SC were also led by a record-setting defense that only let the ball into the back of the net 25 times in an entire campaign. That success was rewarded with all three finalists for Defender of the Year coming from Nashville, but it's their newest signing that took home the award. Joining from Norwegian club Fredrikstad ahead of the season, Woledzi didn't need much time to adjust, appearing in every regular-season match and helping Nashville build from the back to maintain possession. It was a team effort to tie the MLS clean sheet record with 17 during the season, so it's only natural for one of their defenders to get the honors this season.

Jalen Brunson ‘can’t believe’ he has to try to end Taylor Swift-Knicks controversy

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Jalen Brunson is looking to put the Taylor Swift-Knicks fan narrative to bed.

Five months after the mega-pop star's Game 4 NBA Finals appearance and weeks following Tyler Kolek's jab, the Knicks star and Finals MVP told Us Magazine that Swift is "obviously" welcome at Madison Square Garden.

"I can't believe I have to say this," he said. "But I didn't say what was said. Being one of the leaders of the team — the captain of the team — I guess I now have to help somebody out. Yes, she's a member of the Knicks fandom. Yes, she is."

Singer Taylor Swift celebrates with friends during Game 4 of the NBA Finals at Madison Square Garden in New York, Wednesday, June 10, 2026. Jason Szenes for the New York Post

Debate over Swift's NBA loyalty began when she was spotted at Game 3 of the Eastern Conference finals in Cleveland alongside her Ohio-born husband, Travis Kelce.

She was not wearing Knicks gear at the game as Kelce cheered on the Cavaliers — on their way to getting swept by the Knicks.

Weeks later Swift made an appearance at Game 4 of the Finals, witnessing an historic 29-point comeback and OG Anunoby's game-winning tip-in, while wearing a blue "Stevie Knicks" shirt alongside Mariska Hargitay and Haim sisters. Alana and Este.

The 14-time Grammy Award-winner cheered wildly with several viral reactions throughout the thrilling night.

Months later — and after Swift and Kelce's wedding at Madison Square Garden on July 3 — Kolek brought life to the debate again when he answered that Swift was the "fakest" celebrity fan of the newly crowned champions.

When asked the question during an NBA 2K TikTok livestream, the backup guard said: "At the Knicks games, I would say it's very performative because they just want to be seen at the game. They're not necessarily fans."

Singer Taylor Swift walks to her seat before Game 4 of the NBA Finals at Madison Square Garden in New York, Wednesday, June 10, 2026. Charles Wenzelberg / New York Post

"Maybe Taylor Swift? I don't know if I've seen her at any other games," he added. "I guess she has a lot going on so she can't come all the time. But it's a little performative."

Following the comment, Swifties called out Kolek's playing time — or lack thereof — during the Finals.

"Tyler Kolek and Taylor Swift played the same amount of minutes in the 2026 NBA Finals," one fan wrote on X.

"If there's one thing Taylor and tyler have in common is that they were both sitting court side during the finals," another said.

New York Knicks guard Jalen Brunson (11) reacts after hitting a three-point shot against the Indiana Pacers on Sunday, Nov. 22, 2026, at MetLife Stadium. Jason Szenes for NY Post

A Knicks spokesperson released a statement in support of Swift's fandom.

"Taylor Swift has been a Knicks fan for many years, has a long history with Madison Square Garden, and we are incredibly appreciative of her support of the organization," the statement read.

Days later, Kolek apologized for the comment and admitted he is a fan.

"It's just a question that was asked for me, that maybe I answered in the wrong way. Take that on the chin," Kolek said. "I love her music. We were just playing it five minutes ago, and I'm singing every word to every song. Nothing against her at all.

The Knicks (11-6) host the Cavaliers on Wednesday night followed by the Heat and Hawks throughout the Thanksgiving weekend.

The defending champs are riding a five-game winning streak after taking down the Pacers, 113-106, on Sunday.

How Arsenal will change tactics to suit Kepa in goalkeeper’s last chance to impress

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Your weekly briefing on Arsenal, featuring team news and expert analysis from Matt Verri

I would like to be emailed about offers, event and updates from Evening Standard. Read our privacy notice.

Any and all updates on David Raya were welcome if you were an Arsenal fan.

There were the attempts to lip read what head physio Simon Murphy said as he walked off the St James’ Park pitch with Raya.

Then the sighting of Raya the following day in Oxford Circus with his arm in a sling. And the report that he had been able to drive himself home after the club returned to London from Newcastle.

The sense of panic was understandable. Raya is Arsenal’s most irreplaceable player, pivotal to the team not just because of his shot-stopping, but the way he commands his area and how central he is to the build-up in possession as well.

It was good news, then, that he has avoided a serious injury. Raya has not yet been ruled out of facing Manchester City this weekend.

Even with the outlook more positive than it might have been, Arsenal must still find a way to cope without the Spaniard for at least tonight’s match against Borussia Dortmund.

This will be a big night for Kepa Arrizabalaga. He started against Kairat Almaty last season, but that dead rubber aside has not played in the Champions League since making four appearances for Real Madrid in the competition in 2023.

This is a chance to prove he still belongs at this level. Kepa will surely still have an eye on becoming a first-choice goalkeeper again at a top club elsewhere in the seasons ahead. However, that kind of evidence has been in short supply since he arrived at Arsenal.

Mikel Arteta put his faith in Kepa last season in the Carabao Cup final and it was not repaid. Kepa was booked for hauling down Jeremy Doku after misjudging a bouncing ball, before gifting City the lead when spilling a cross.

His distribution, too, was poor. Throughout the match, Arsenal’s build-up was almost entirely nullified by City and that started with the goalkeeper.

A key facet of Arsenal’s build-up is Raya’s ability to not just go short, but to ping flat, long passes up the pitch to play over the press when required.

It is easy to forget just how highly rated Kepa once was, he remains the most expensive goalkeeper signing of all time

Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund: Champions League - LIVE

Can Nwaneri play against Arsenal in Champions League tonight?

Why isn’t Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund on TNT Sports tonight?

Discover a hidden island paradise in the Indian Ocean

“David Raya can play six, I swear,” Noni Madueke said in a recent interview.

Kepa cannot play those same passes with the same trajectory or accuracy. Nor is he as convincing in inspiring the same confidence when going short under pressure.

Asked on Monday whether Arsenal would have to change how they play without Raya, Arteta said: “We adapt to players’ qualities, and they all have different ones.

“There are certain things that, in our game or in the way that we understand the game and we want to play the game, everybody has to be 100% committed to, but then we make adjustments in relation to the qualities of the players.”

One of those adjustments will have to be how Arsenal build up. Illan Meslier would be more comfortable in that respect, with the ball at his feet, but he is liable to make big mistakes and can be exposed when the ball is crossed on top of him.

There has never been any real consideration from Arteta or his coaching staff that it would be Meslier getting the nod over Kepa to stand in for Raya.

When he signed in the summer, Meslier was aware of the pecking order. He arrived primarily to allow Tommy Setford to go out on loan, rather than to replace Kepa as No2.

Meslier started in the Carabao Cup defeat to Fleetwood, even with Kepa fit and in the squad. However, that was due to a desire to reward Meslier for his consistency in training and impact on the squad, not a rejigging of the pecking order.

It is easy to forget just how highly rated Kepa once was. He remains the most expensive goalkeeper signing of all time, joining Chelsea for £72million in 2018.

That price tag has followed him around ever since. So too Carabao Cup finals, both last season’s horror show and the 2019 edition when he refused to be substituted.

The temptation is to say a quiet night, one in which he is not noticed against Dortmund, would be ideal for Kepa, but Raya does not have many of those.

Even when he is not required to pull off saves, he is still making line-breaking passes or pushing up close to the halfway line to snuff out attacks.

Kepa failed on all those counts in the Carabao Cup final last season. Another poor performance this time and it is hard to see how he can retain Arteta’s trust.

In his biggest test since that Wembley occasion, Arsenal need Kepa to minimise the disruption of Raya’s absence.

Why isn’t Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund on TNT Sports tonight? Champions League TV schedule change explained

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Your weekly briefing on Arsenal, featuring team news and expert analysis from Matt Verri

I would like to be emailed about offers, event and updates from Evening Standard. Read our privacy notice.

Arsenal take on Borussia Dortmund in the Champions League this evening, but it will not be shown on TNT Sports, as is usually the case for European football in the UK.

How Arsenal will change tactics to suit Kepa in his last chance to impress

Can Nwaneri play against Arsenal in Champions League tonight?

Arsenal XI vs Borussia Dortmund: Predicted lineup and confirmed team news

Discover a hidden island paradise in the Indian Ocean

The Gunners go into this game looking to get back to winning ways after a surprise draw with Newcastle at St James’ Park, where the north Londoners were given an almighty scare.

Noni Madueke netted a late equaliser for the Premier League champions, who had fallen behind after Malick Thiaw bundled home from a set piece after Harvey Barnes had cancelled out Eberechi Eze’s opener.

They take on a Dortmund side that are top of the Bundesliga after 10 matches, and they are two points ahead of Klassiker rivals Bayern Munich.

Amongst Niko Kovac’s cohort is Ethan Nwaneri, who is on loan at the German side from Arsenal, and he is eligible to face his parent club tonight due to Champions League rules permitting loan players to be available to face the teams they are contracted to.

Arsenal vs Borussia Dortmund will not be broadcast live in the UK on TNT Sports. That is because under the current Champions League rights agreement on these shores, Amazon Prime Video has the first pick of one Tuesday night fixture to show exclusively on their platform up until the semi-finals.

This week they have chosen the encounter at the Emirates Stadium, which kicks off at 8pm GMT at Old Trafford. Coverage on Amazon begins at 6:30pm, hosted by Gabby Logan.

Amazon’s current arrangement will continue from 2027-2031 as per the terms of the next Champions League TV rights deal, which will see the bulk of matches head to US streaming giants Paramount in an agreement struck in 2025 worth well over £1billion.

Highlights will remain on the BBC, with Sky Sports buying the rights to show all Europa League and Conference League games in the UK from next season.

Sinner surgery casts doubt over Australian Open appearance

📅 Nov 24, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Jannik Sinner has successfully undergone surgery on his right knee; casting doubt over his Australian Open participation; You can watch the Next Gen ATP Finals from 9-13 December in Reggio Calabria, Italy live on Sky Sports Tennis

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

Former world No 1 Jannik Sinner has undergone surgery on his troublesome knee, casting doubt over his hopes of playing at the Australian Open.

The 25-year-old has not played since ​winning his fifth Grand Slam ‌title at Wimbledon in July.

The Italian world No 2 ​said this month he was confident of returning in time for the Australian Open, which starts on January ​17, but would not rush his ⁠recovery.

Sky Italy are reporting he could be out for up to three months.

Five-time Grand Slam champion Sinner posted on social media: "Over the past few months, together with my team we've tried everything to treat my injury without surgery. After some very encouraging progress early on, my knee unfortunately didn't respond as we'd all hoped, that's why today I underwent a minor surgery to improve the condition of my iliotibial band.

"I really miss being on court and will now begin my recovery process together with my team. Thank you for all your support. See you soon."

Sinner set a new record by winning the first five Masters 1000 tournaments of the year, becoming only the second man after Novak Djokovic to complete the career Golden Masters.

But he appeared exhausted after suffering a shock early loss at the French Open before rebounding strongly at Wimbledon.

His absence has allowed Alexander Zverev to overtake him at the top of the rankings, while Carlos Alcaraz is now close behind in third after a hugely impressive comeback from his own injury issues.

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

Sinner was operated on in Turku, on the southwest coast of Finland, by renowned surgeon Lasse Lempainen.

The procedure involved lengthening the iliotibial band through arthroscopy, using small Z-shaped incisions to reduce tension in the area.

His knee will initially be immobilised before he begins intensive physiotherapy to regain mobility and strength, with his return to full fitness for 2027 unclear.

Sinner said he had lost a significant amount of weight after having his ⁠tonsils removed and was in ​the process of rebuilding muscle.

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

He recently ​parted ways with his Australian ​coach Darren Cahill, with Swede Magnus Norman ⁠joining his coaching setup.

Seven-time Grand Slam champion Carlos Alcaraz, who won the ATP Finals ⁠title for the first time ​on Sunday, is the defending Australian ​Open champion.

Stream tennis with no contract on NOW and the Sky Sports App. You can watch the Next Gen ATP Finals from 9-13 December in Reggio Calabria, Italy live on Sky Sports Tennis.
